# Independent LI6 derivation: A01–A04 and LA01–LA03

This argument uses the original physical source and complete scalar reference.
The fresh finite calculations are specified in `rebuild.py` and recorded in
the successful attempt named in `REPORT.md`. The copied original programs
were read as definitions; none is executed or imported. Finite sums below
are exact exterior-algebra identities, not field truncations. Numerical
claims are machine-readable in `BOUNDS.json` in that attempt.

## 1. Full source, flavor and common original receiving map (A01)

Number cube sites and Walsh indices by 0,...,7, and set
chi_k(x)=(-1)^popcount(k&x). Every physical creation mode is 4x+a, where
a=0,1,2,3 means p-up,p-down,n-up,n-down. Exterior words carry the sign of
their permutation to this site-major order. The fixed population is
mu=(2,1,2,1); its one-cell space has dimension
C(8,2)8 C(8,2)8=50176 inside the full exterior power of dimension C(32,6).

Read all 24 columns of the actual adjacent-pair injection J. A column is
the ordered pair (x,y), with x xor y in {1,2,4}. With Q removing Walsh0,
the Pauli-allowed residual A=(Q tensor Q)J has normalized Walsh entries

    A_(k,l),(x,y)=chi_k(x)chi_l(y)/8, k,l=1,...,7.

G00=A* A is positive definite of rank24. The replay reconstructs it and
compares it to the old metric after matching both placement labels. This
is important because the historical metric and actual injection do not
use the same column ordering. Independently choosing down-core Walsh
modes a,b=0,...,4, with up0 occupied in each species, gives the raw map R
with R*R=I25 tensor G00. Thus F0=R(I25 tensor G00^(-1/2)) is one rank600
isometry, without separate column normalization.

The uniform pair coordinate u0=1/sqrt(24) and the physical offset contrast
u1=(1_(y=x xor1)-1_(y=x xor2))/4 are orthonormal and have G00 eigenvalues
5/8 and1. Their polar images, derived from A, are

    u=sum_(k=1)^7 (3-2|k|)|k,k>/sqrt(15),
    v=sum_(k=1)^7 [chi_k(1)-chi_k(2)]|k,k>/4.

For proton down excitations 1,2,3,4 the phase edges are 0,2,3,1; for
neutron excitations they are 5,6,8,4. Edge7 uses v and both down cores0.
The physical join edges therefore retain their down-Walsh4 excitation;
they are not replaced with a free register. Each geminal term is the
signed word (p_up0,p_upk,p_downa,n_up0,n_upk,n_downb). The replay forms
all nine words and recovers the phase Gram I9. The original address rule
gives q=(0,0,2,2,2,0,0,0,0) for14336, hence

    z=(1,1,-1,-1,-1,1,1,1,1)/3.

The corresponding 18 real/imaginary coordinates are compared directly
with the sealed original preparation.

For completeness, the representation statement follows by applying
ordinary Schur–Weyl decomposition to the two tensor factors of
(C8 tensor C4)^tensor6 and selecting the sign representation of the
diagonal permutation action. The multiplicity of sign in [lambda]
tensor[gamma] is one for gamma=lambda^t and zero otherwise (tensoring a
permutation irrep with sign conjugates its partition). Therefore

    wedge6(C8 tensor C4)= direct_sum_lambda O_lambda tensor F_(lambda^t).

The orbital and flavor bilinears commute by CAR. Consequently an orbital
map U_lambda tensor identity preserves every full-flavor matrix element,
including multiplicity coherences, not just sector probabilities.

The replay constructs C2=sum_ab Q_ab Q_ba and
C3=sum_abc Q_ab Q_bc Q_ca with full intermediate flavor weights. Acting
on the Ferrers determinant derives each central label. A Hermitian
combination L=100 C2+C3 has distinct labels on all nine allowed
partitions; its rational Lagrange polynomials are the projectors.
Each complete density fiber is enumerated before these projectors are
formed. Adjointness, idempotence, eigen-equations and completeness are
checked exactly. Tableaux independently give flavor-weight dimensions,
and the dimension sum over all orbital irreps is50176.

The nonzero phase sectors have labels and dimensions

| Orbital label | Flavor shape | C2 | C3 | mu multiplicity | phase rank |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
|42|2211|14|46|1|5|
|33|222|18|54|1|4|
|321|321|24|111|4|8|

The real C2 degeneracy with411 (C2=18,C3=90) is resolved, not discarded.
For any paired proton/neutron excitation the complete projected Grams are

    G42=(47/150) [[1,1],[1,1]],
    G33=(17/90) [[1,-1],[-1,1]],
    G321=(1/225) [[112,-28],[-28,112]].

Cross-excitation blocks vanish, edge7 is pure42, and other phase sector
Grams are zero. These statements are contractions of the complete CAR
projectors, not an assumed classical mixture. Contraction with z gives

    w42=169/675, w33=34/135, w321=112/225.

An image of phase rank r with flavor-weight multiplicity m can have r m
independent orbital coefficient vectors: expand each source vector in
one common flavor basis and span all its coefficients. The needed orbital
capacities are therefore at most5,4,32. Rank8 alone is not sufficient
in321. The original target profiles are all ordered adjacent4+2 pairs,
unordered adjacent3+3 pairs, and wedges3x+2y+z with distinct neighbors
y,z of x. They supply respectively24,12,48 orthogonal orbital lines.
Each line is a permutation of the highest orbital weight and carries its
entire flavor factor. Exact fiber ranks in mu are1,1,4 at every profile.

Choose a basis of each complete source orbital coefficient span, map it
isometrically to that target list, and complete both lists to a basis of
O_lambda. Acting as U_lambda tensor identity gives one whole-parent
unitary U. Use identity on phase-absent irreps. This finite construction
specifies a common map on all600 source directions without forming a
dense50176 matrix. Its existence and flavor preservation follow from
basis completion and the representation decomposition, not a numerical
search. The original source metric and full phase/flavor correlations
are unchanged.

This choice can be made a fully specified finite algorithm. Order the
canonical exterior basis lexicographically. For each flavor highest weight,
solve its weight and Q_(a,a+1)-annihilation equations over the rationals,
then apply lexicographic Gram–Schmidt with positive square roots. Ordered
flavor-lowering words construct one flavor basis with a common Gram for
every orbital copy. Expand every projected source column in that basis
and retain the orbital coefficient for every flavor-weight vector. Their
lexicographic Gram–Schmidt list defines S_lambda. Order target profiles
lexicographically and identify their flavor factors using orbital
permutations, which commute with every flavor operation. Map the S list
to the first target lines, and complete both orbital lists against the
fixed orbital basis by the same Gram–Schmidt rule. Define U_lambda by
those two complete lists, and identity on phase-absent irreps. Thus a
single basis completion specifies all600 source images and every
flavor correlation; separate column choices are never used. These are
algebraic instructions for the existence proof, and do not require the
replay to construct a dense full-parent matrix.

## 2. Full Fock law and coherent eigen-embedding (A01–A02)

The fixed Hamiltonian on the infinite cell lattice is

    H=T+dGamma(omega)+g rho^T(b+b^dagger),
    T=sum_particles[2 tau L_cube+t_cell L_Z3],
    omega=direct_sum_cells(I+L_cube).

There is no density counterterm, and the field vacuum energy is zero in
each branch. At fixed fermion number N, T is bounded and nonnegative,
I<=omega<=7I, and ||rho_D||<=N uniformly in the matter determinant D.
The annihilation estimate bounds the linear field term by an arbitrarily
small multiple of dGamma(omega) plus a constant. This gives the common
closed semibounded form on D(N_b^(1/2)). Uniform Weyl estimates for
f_D=-g omega^(-1)rho_D preserve that form domain. Completing the square
on the finite matter/finite field core, then by closure, yields

    H=T+(b+gG rho)^dagger omega(b+gG rho)-g^2 V(rho),
    V(rho)=sum_cells rho_cell^T G rho_cell,
    G=(I+L_cube)^(-1).

The cube inverse is reconstructed by exact elimination and independently
by its Walsh spectrum. Its distance entries are (36,13,8,6)/105.
On the three target profile families its potentials are
v=(928/105,882/105,770/105). For every target branch n use its full
coherent state |f_n>, not a finite boson approximation. The field energy
is +g^2 v and the vertex is -2g^2 v. Their complete sum is -g^2 v.

Write D0=36(tau+t_cell), H0=D0+field+vertex, K=H-H0. K contains only
the actual off-diagonal internal and cell hopping, with entries -2 tau
and -t_cell times their CAR signs. The equilibrium-cloud embedding J
of each full target factor satisfies H0 J=E J, E=D0-g^2 v. Its target
norm is exactly identity because distinct matter determinants remain
orthogonal even when their coherent clouds overlap.

Every target density in42 has even cube count parity; those in33/321
have odd parity. Each nearest-neighbor internal or cell hop changes
the full checkerboard parity. A six-particle same-cell translation adds
6 times the cell parity and leaves it unchanged. Therefore

    J* K J=0, J* K^3 J=0.

The latter is an all-space parity identity, including mixed internal/cell
paths. The field/coherent overlap does not change matter parity. With

    V=J* K^2 J, B=J* K(H-E)K J,

it follows that B=J* K(H0-E)K J exactly. Internal and single-cell-escape
final determinants are orthogonal; H0 preserves those matter addresses.

## 3. Every density image and the signed energy moment (A02)

Let a_(m,i) be the signed adjacency coefficient from target i to final
matter determinant m, in the canonical site-major basis. Rebuild every
m before contracting. The field overlap is

    c_ij=exp[-(g^2/2)||G(rho_i-rho_j)||^2].

The exact full-Fock matrix element, divided by g^2, is

    q_(m,ij)c_ij,
    q_(m,ij)=v+rho_i^T G rho_j
                 -rho_m^T G rho_i-rho_m^T G rho_j.

Indeed <f_i|dGamma(omega)|f_j>=f_i^T omega f_j c_ij and
the vertex contributes g rho_m^T(f_i+f_j)c_ij; D0-E=g^2v.
Thus the two ordered vertex terms are separately required. There is
no missing field self term or kinetic diagonal in this equation.

The reconstructed internal matrices are

    N_ij=sum_m a_(m,i)a_(m,j)c_ij,
    Q_ij=sum_m a_(m,i)a_(m,j)q_(m,ij)c_ij,
    V_internal=4 tau^2 N, B_internal=4 tau^2 g^2 Q.

An independently associated contraction uses raw N0=A* A,
D_x=A* diag(rho_m,x)A and p_i=G rho_i:

    Q0_ij=(v+rho_i^T G rho_j)N0_ij
                      -sum_x(p_i,x+p_j,x)(D_x)_ij.

The replay retains all eight D_x, every signed image row, each ordered
field/vertex piece, each exponent class, and the exact residual of these
two associations. Its density images precede compression.

For the diagonal, a target has respectively14,12,12 legal hops: start
with6 times3 and remove each doubly occupied flavor edge in both
directions. Moreover

    sum_hops q_(m,ii)=2 rho_i^T L G rho_i
                    =2(||rho_i||^2-v).

Blocked hops cancel in opposite directions, so this sum formula remains
valid with CAR blocking. It gives the exact Q diagonals
2344/105,96/5,40/3.

All mixed terms can be classified by two hops. For42 only reversing the
ordered edge connects distinct targets. The two hopping orders add,
giving a signed coefficient2. Its intermediate density is the average
of the two source densities, hence q=0 in each order. The source-density
difference is2(e_x-e_y), giving exponent2332/11025.
For33, distinct target determinants cannot be connected by two hops.

For321 write a target as(x,y,z), with y,z distinct neighbors of x.
The norm mixed partner is (y,x,z xor x xor y): moving the singly
present flavor from x to y and the parallel residual particle from z to
z xor x xor y gives two orders. Their signed norm contributions add,
and their energy pieces cancel because the intermediate densities sum
to rho_i+rho_j. Its exponent is34/225. There is also a two-step
single-particle displacement from z to the third neighbor w of x,
keeping x,y fixed. One path goes through occupied x and one through
the opposite square corner. Their CAR signs oppose, so N cancels.
Their energy weights differ by4/5, and exponent is17/225.
Swapping y,z has equal energy weights and cancels in both moments.
These exhaust the same-flavor two-step and two-single-edge changes
between the prescribed nested-flavor target determinants. The complete
enumeration supplies an exact additional check of this classification.

Consequently, with signed permutation involutions S,T in the canonical
basis (retained explicitly in `MOMENTS.json`),

|Sector|N|Q|
|---|---|---|
|42|14I+2 r42 S|(2344/105)I|
|33|12I|(96/5)I|
|321|12I+2 r^2 S|(40/3)I+(4/5)r T|

Here r42=exp(-2332g^2/11025), r=exp(-17g^2/225).
The underlying permutations square to identity, and in321 the edge
reflection defining S commutes with exchange of the two remaining
neighbor directions defining T. The CAR basis signs are the signs
of the corresponding exterior intertwiners, so the same involution
and commutation identities hold with those signs. The replay checks
S*=S, S^2=I, T*=T, T^2=I and ST=TS exactly. An explicit diagonal
basis gauge relates the newly constructed site-major forms to the
submitted flavor-major forms; neither a sign nor a source column is
discarded in the comparison.

There are312,144,432 distinct internal image determinants. Cell escape
has36 outcomes per target determinant: six particles times six adjacent
cells. Its final remote address uniquely recovers the original determinant
and the moved particle, so there are no cross-column terms. For a hop
from site x, the old cloud's cost above E is2g^2(G rho_i)_x. Summing

    V_cell=36 t_cell^2 I,
    B_cell=12g^2 v t_cell^2 I.

The replay explicitly forms all864,432,1728 cell image rows, including

## 4. Common graph polar map, source extension and energy (A01,A03,A04)

For real a>0 put

    C=(I+a^2 V)^(-1/2), J_a=(J-aKJ)C.

Since J*KJ=0, J_a*J_a=C(I+a^2V)C=I on the whole target factor.
Expanding its H compression and using H0J=EJ gives

    J_a* H J_a=E I+C(-2a V+a^2 B)C.                 (1)

This formula includes the K^3 term through the proved parity zero.
All hopping is bounded at fixed particle number. Each of the finitely
many source clouds is in the field energy domain, so these products
and finite matrix elements are legitimate on the full Fock space.

Here V is strictly positive. Set F=KJ V^(-1/2); F is an isometry
orthogonal to J. On the paired spaces J and F the block rotation

    [[C, a sqrt(V) C],[-a sqrt(V) C,C]]

is unitary and sends J to J_a. Take identity on its orthogonal
complement. The matrices act on the orbital factor and are identity
on the complete flavor factor, so this is a U4-equivariant joint
unitary. Apply it after the original matter U and Weyl dressing to
the whole source600. Therefore the resulting map is common on every
source direction, even those whose energy is not under discussion.

For every original source-metric unitary Write w, in polar coordinates
the transported physical unitary is

    J_new w J_new*+(I-J_new J_new*).

It preserves all source overlaps and its original action. A transported
Write need not remain a local diagonal phase operation. No separately
normalized columns, heralded normalization, or imaginary-time operation
is declared deterministic and free.

If V>=v_minus I and B<=b_plus I, b_plus>=0, the energy correction
in(1) is at most

    -G I, G=(2a v_minus-a^2 b_plus)/(1+a^2 v_minus).

To see this without assuming V and B commute, first replace B by
b_plus I inside the congruence. The resulting scalar function of V
is decreasing, because the derivative of the positive gain is
(2a+a^4 b_plus)/(1+a^2 v)^2>0. Functional calculus then uses its
smallest permitted v. This proves the bound on every vector of that
target orbital/flavor factor, including entangled flavor coefficients.
Its weighted contraction on the unchanged z uses exactly the original
three weights, not a new source or a scalar sector replacement.

## 5. Complete proper reference and benchmark strict margin (A03)

The full Fock square gives H>=T-g^2 V. Dropping nonnegative cell
kinetic leaves a direct sum over cell flavor allocations. For a fragment
count m<=mu, set N=|m|, k=number of active flavors. Site occupation is
at most k and N<=2k. Hence

    105V<=13N^2+23 sum_x rho_x^2
         <=13N^2+23[k^2+(N-k)^2].                 (2)

This is attained in one cell by all active flavors at x, with all repeated
flavors at one adjacent site. It covers every physical flavor allocation.
The replay enumerates all60712 determinants in34 nonempty proper count
sectors, rebuilding their complete potential histograms.

For m=(2,1,1,1) or its allowed flavor exchange, the complete maximum
space has24 states, potential716/105, next gap40/105, and PTP=30 tau P.
Each maximum has13 legal hops. Different dense vertices cannot meet
in two hops; at a common dense vertex the two residual-neighbor paths
have opposite CAR signs. Thus PTQTP=52 tau^2 P. The full one-cell
Pauli floor on Q is4 tau. For1111, its eight maximum states have
potential576/105, gap138/105, PTP=24 tau P and PTQTP=48 tau^2 P,
with QTQ>=0. All these exact finite matrices are independently rebuilt.

# LI6 hypotheses and exact scope

These are the frozen scientific premises, not additional admission criteria.

| Premise | Use | Breaking variation / retained boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Six CAR fermions, four flavors, population (2,1,2,1); full exterior parent | Source rank, flavor commutant, every density/hopping image | Removing 321 loses actual source norm; the weight sector has dimension 50176 in ambient dimension 906192. |
| Original ordered adjacent24 injection, Pauli Q on both up orbitals, independent down cores 0 through 4 | Raw metric I25 tensor G00, full polar rank600 | No columnwise normalization, old blocked register, or convenient subsource substitution. |
| Original ledger order, physical join down mode4, address14336, norm9 | Actual phase9 and unchanged normalized preparation | Weighted gain is on this input. The graph map is defined more widely; its weighted upper is not uniform600. |
| Full U4 equivariance and skew Howe decomposition | Carry all flavor vectors, including multiplicity4 in321 | Capacity uses phase rank times flavor-weight dimension, not just scalar weights. |
| T=sum(2 tau L_cube+t_cell L_cells), omega=I+L_cube per cell, linear total-density vertex, vacuum zero, no density counterterm | Full Fock square, potentials, proper lower, diagonal and off-diagonal kinetic payments | Tau=1, t_cell=1/1000, g^2=35 for numerical margins; same parameters and reference on both branches. |
| Full symmetric Fock space; real normalizable source clouds of finite field-energy norm | H0J=EJ, all coherent self and vertex contractions, exact overlaps | No field-number cutoff. Diagonal source field energy +g^2v and vertex -2g^2v are both retained. |
| Physical internal and nearest-cell CAR hopping | V, B, parity and escape terms | Escape contributes 36 t_cell^2 and 12 g^2 v t_cell^2. Dropping it changes both exact moments. |
| One common real positive-polar graph filter on each complete target factor | Isometry and exact energy pullback | A real-time hopping pulse has a different tangent. An abstract unitary extension does not price a physical implementation. |
| Same static G, independent real passive harmonic law with its own normal ordered vacuum and normalizable equilibrium cloud | Conservative family theorem; coherent overlaps in [0,1] | A changed cloud covariance must be used. Arbitrary added instantaneous potentials, complex geometric phases, non-Gaussian fields, or a changed reference are not included. |
| Proper matter breakup comparison over all flavor partitions and all cell refinements | Common lower -752g^2/105+8 tau when g^2/tau>=735/44 | Not an identification of essential spectrum or a source-specific fiber threshold. Escaping bosons are an additional spectral issue. |

The same-static family is stated concretely as a per-cell real harmonic Fock
law H_f=dGamma(Omega), vertex g rho^T h(b+b^dagger), with
h Omega^(-1) h^T=G. Each f_rho=-g Omega^(-1)h^T rho must lie in the
one-particle space and its energy form domain. This includes every finite
real member of the supplied canonical H_s family with cloud metric
K^(-(s+3)/2), normal ordered in that member's own vacuum. Cell fields and
the complete scalar reference retain their original independence convention.

Analytic evidence supplies the all-cell, full-Fock, representation, unitary
extension and all-family arguments. Exact host evidence supplies finite
source/CAR/projector identities, all proper flavor counts, density images,
and rational margins. Arb supplies directed exponential and two-law cloud
checks. Earlier proofs and tables are comparison operands, not a substitute

The open items remain physical variational-filter/controller implementation,

fiber/cluster/escaping-boson theorem. The 321 sector is not spectrally promoted

# N12 — complete reconstruction of B01–B04

The new twelve-particle construction gives the submitted strict variational
comparison against every proper particle/flavor fragment channel under its
specified scalar law. The selected success branch is part of a coherent
source-preserving joint instrument; the complete instrument, its conditional
energy claim, and a supplied-bare-target loading stage are different objects.
The next use is a source-explicit case review and public replay. No physical
formation operation or manuscript change follows automatically.

All computed values and finite lists below are in
`attempts/003/output/{occupancy,targets,flavor_source,energy_loading,controls}.json`.
The fixed numerical assertions were supplied as claims, not desired outcomes.
The first execution's kinetic-sign error and its traceback remain in
`attempts/001`. `attempts/002` is the corrected successful predecessor.
The implementation is new; no earlier scientific runner was executed.

## 1. Exact physical model and full Fock meaning

Let sites be `(R,x)`, with `R in Z^3` and `x in {0,...,7}` the binary cube.
There are four CAR flavors in the order `(p-up,p-down,n-up,n-down)`.
The benchmark total population is `mu=(4,2,4,2)` and `N=12`.
The full fixed-weight one-cell matter space has dimension

\[
 {8\choose4}{8\choose2}{8\choose4}{8\choose2}=3,841,600.
\]

It is a subspace of the full global exterior power, not a definition by the
selected target coordinates. The scalar field has its full symmetric Fock
space over all cell/site modes. Write

\[
 T=d\Gamma_{\mathrm{CAR}}(2\tau L_{Q_3}+t_cL_{\mathbb Z^3}),\quad
 \omega=\bigoplus_R(I+L_{Q_3}),\quad
 H=T+d\Gamma(\omega)+g\sum_{R,x}\rho_{R,x}(b_{R,x}+b^\dagger_{R,x}).
\]

Here `tau,t_c>=0`, `g` is real, and the vacuum is normally ordered to zero
in every cell on every branch. There is no density counterterm. Exact
inversion gives

\[
 G=(I+L_{Q_3})^{-1},\qquad
 105G_{xy}=(36,13,8,6)_{d(x,y)}.
\]

The runner checks `(I+L) (105G)=105I` directly. The Walsh frequencies are
`1+2|q|`, including their degeneracies, hence `1<=omega<=7`.

On the fixed-N sector, the vector density has norm at most `N`; its entries
commute. The field interaction is infinitesimally form bounded with respect
to `dGamma(omega)`: the usual creation/annihilation estimates, `omega>=I`,
and the bounded density vector give this immediately. The bounded finite-N
lattice kinetic term does not change the field form domain. On that closed
form domain,

\[
 H=T+\|\omega^{1/2}b+g\omega^{-1/2}\rho\|^2-g^2 V,
 \qquad V=\sum_R\rho_R^TG\rho_R.                 \tag{1}
\]

For a matter determinant `D`, put `f_D=-gG rho_D` in every occupied cell.
This is a square-summable normal-Fock displacement: both its norm and its
field-energy norm are uniformly bounded at fixed `N`. The decomposable
Weyl map `W_g=sum_D |D><D| tensor W(f_D)` is unitary, commutes with full
global flavor, and preserves the form domain. On its vacuum images the
square in (1) has zero mean. Separately,

\[
 E_{\rm field}=+g^2V,\qquad E_{\rm vertex}=-2g^2V.
                                                        \tag{2}
\]

Thus the net attraction includes the field self energy, all particle self
terms and both ordered mixed density terms. Neither full Fock nor a positive
field complement is discarded.

## 2. B01: every density, flavor, cell and proper fragment

A determinant in one cell has occupancies `r_x in {0,1,2,3,4}` and numerator

\[
 Q(r)=105r^TGr=\sum_x36r_x^2+
              2\sum_{x<y}(105G_{xy})r_xr_y.              \tag{3}
\]

The runner branches on every one of five choices at each of eight sites.
When appending `n` at site `x`, it adds
`36 n^2 + 2n sum_(y<x) (105G_xy) r_y`. Induction on the prefix proves that
this enumerates every occupancy exactly once and evaluates (3) exactly.
There are `5^8` leaves; all classes through total population twelve are
retained, with every geometric value and a maximizing witness. Sorting is
used only as a key after evaluating the actual geometry. The count is 141
classes, not a restriction to the target shape.

For flavor subcount `m`, realization of `r` is a binary 4-by-8 incidence
matrix with row sums `m` and column sums `r`. Here is a constructive proof
of the feasibility tests. In a network, use source-to-flavor capacities
`m_a`, flavor-to-site capacities one, and site-to-sink capacities `r_x`.
For a cut containing flavor subset `A`, minimize over the site side. Its
minimum capacity is

\[
 \sum_{a\notin A}m_a+\sum_x\min(|A|,r_x).
\]

It is at least `sum m` precisely when
`sum_(a in A)m_a <= sum_x min(|A|,r_x)`. For fixed `|A|=k`, the largest
left side uses the `k` largest row sums. These are the four row cuts.
Transpose the same network to get the eight column cuts

\[
 \sum_{x=1}^{\ell}r_x^\downarrow
       \leq\sum_a\min(\ell,m_a),\quad 1\leq\ell\leq8. \tag{4}
\]

Equal total is also required. Integral augmenting paths yield a binary
incidence when the maximum flow has that total. If augmentation stops
early, the residual reachable set is a smaller cut, proving infeasibility.
This proves necessity and sufficiency without importing a numerical degree
heuristic. Fresh flow, row-cut and column-cut results agree on every relevant
pair. There are 2,164 nonzero comparisons plus the zero-population check.
The maximizing incidence witness for each flavor count is retained.

Let `M(m)` be the largest legal single-cell numerator and define, in
increasing total population,

\[
 W(0)=0,\quad
 W(m)=\max\{M(m),\ \max_{0<a<m}[W(a)+W(m-a)]\}.         \tag{5}
\]

Inequalities between multi-indices are componentwise, with both pieces
nonempty. A finite-N configuration has at most N occupied cells. It either
uses one cell or has a nonempty first cell and a smaller remainder. This
proves the upper direction of (5) by induction. Conversely, each term is
realized by putting its incidence witnesses in distinct cells, because the
static field kernel has no cross-cell entries. Hence (5) is the exact
static maximum over all cell allocations, including arbitrarily remote
cells, not a cell cutoff.

For a proper fragment partition, combine its first block `a` and the
remainder `mu-a`. Every further split and every cell allocation is already
allowed in the two W factors. Conversely each binary split is a proper
partition. Thus the exact all-proper static maximum is

\[
 P(\mu)=\max_{0<a<\mu}\{W(a)+W(\mu-a)\}.                \tag{6}
\]

All 225 flavor subvectors and all 223 ordered nontrivial splits are retained.
The maxima by smaller fragment size `1,2,3,4,5,6` are, respectively,
`2340,2192,2004,1960,1836,1856`. The worst `11+1` split includes
`(4,2,3,2)+(0,0,1,0)`; its reverse and the proton-up counterpart also occur.
Equations (1), (5), (6) and `T>=0` give the complete proper comparison

\[
 L_{\rm proper}\geq-\frac{2340}{105}g^2.
                                                        \tag{7}
\]

At the submitted benchmark it is `-780`. This is a bound for proper
matter-particle/flavor comparison channels under the given common zero.
It does not identify an essential spectrum. The fixed-mu restriction is
explicit. Full U4 covariance also transports the bound within any selected
global irrep that has this weight; no bound for arbitrary new populations
is inferred by inventory.

## 3. B02: physical targets, all kinetic terms and coherent Gram

Choose two full sites `F` and two disjoint partial sites `U`. Put every
flavor at F and both up flavors `(0,2)` at U. There are
`C(8,2) C(6,2)=420` different Slaters, each with density a permutation of
`(4,4,2,2,0,0,0,0)` and population mu. Select the top 25 by exact (3), with
the lexicographic tie convention in `replay.py`. Their potential minimum
is `2592/105`; 24 are at `2632/105`.

Every site occupancy is even. A legal physical one-particle hop changes two
occupancies from even to odd. It cannot connect any two members of this
whole family. A cell hop leaves the all-twelve-in-one-cell sector. Hence
both internal and intercell off-diagonal kinetic compressions vanish.
All internal first-hop CAR images are rebuilt, including their signs, and
matched to the retained physical operands after an explicit mode-order
change. The source-plus-internal-image basis has 429 rows. It is an image
list, not the physical parent. Its complete first-hop Gram is retained;
the images are not set to zero as operators. Intercell escape has Gram
`72 t_c^2 I25`: each of the twelve particles has six distinct outward
hops, and an escaped configuration has a unique reverse hop to this cell.

The physical internal kinetic diagonal is six tau per particle and the
cell diagonal is six t_c per particle. Thus the complete diagonal remains
`72(tau+t_c) I25`. It has not been subtracted as an energy reference.

Write the selected matter Slaters as `D_i`. Their field-cloud overlap is

\[
 \langle f_i|f_j\rangle
  =\exp[-g^2(\rho_i-\rho_j)^TG^2(\rho_i-\rho_j)/2].     \tag{8}
\]

Every exact exponent, every `rho_i^T G rho_j`, and every
`rho_i^T G^2 rho_j` is retained. The full matter-field Gram is nevertheless
`I25`, since the matter Slaters are orthogonal. Distinct field clouds are
not falsely declared orthogonal. Dressing multiplies a hopping entry by
(8), so the already-zero kinetic compression remains zero. Equations
(1) and (2) therefore give, on the entire selected span,

\[
 J_T^* HJ_T=72(\tau+t_c)I-
               g^2\operatorname{diag}(V_i),\qquad J_T^*J_T=I. \tag{9}
\]

All flavor descendants have the same local densities, so this argument
also covers the full selected flavor copies, not only their highest
fixed-weight columns.

Combining (7) and (9), the exact sufficient complete-energy margin is

\[
 m(g^2,\tau,t_c)=\frac{2592-2340}{105}g^2-72(\tau+t_c)
               =\frac{12}{5}g^2-72(\tau+t_c).            \tag{10}
\]

It is positive for `g^2>30(tau+t_c)`. At `(1,1/1000,35)`, the upper is
`-98991/125` and the margin is `1491/125`. This sufficient comparison is
not an exact physical binding threshold or an optimized complete-energy

## 4. B03: reconstructing the actual contact source, not its inventory

The actual adjacent-pair injection is read from the retained original
64-by-24 matrix J. Each of its columns is an ordered adjacent cube pair
`(x,y)`. In nonzero Walsh indices,

\[
 A_{(k,l),(x,y)}=\chi_k(x)\chi_l(y)/8,\quad k,l=1,...,7.
\]

The exact raw metric is `G00=A* A`. Its positive LDL pivots are retained,
and its rank is 24. The full contact raw source has metric
`M=I25 tensor G00`, rank 600, and polar source `F0=R M^(-1/2)`.
Indeed, for each of the 25 down-core pairs `(a,b) in {0,...,4}^2`, the
two up zero modes and the nonzero residual up modes embed A in an
orthonormal CAR determinant dictionary. Different down-core pairs are
orthogonal because their down-flavor orbitals differ. Canonical creation
signs are changes between orthonormal exterior bases. Consequently the
raw Gram on all 600 columns is exactly `I25 tensor A* A`.
This is one polar map; it is not columnwise normalization.
For the uniform 24-vector and the actual offset contrast the eigenvalues
are `5/8` and `1`, respectively. Their images derived from A are

\[
 u=\sum_{k=1}^7\frac{3-2|k|}{\sqrt{15}}|k,k\rangle,
 \qquad v=\sum_{k=1}^7\frac{\chi_k(1)-\chi_k(2)}4|k,k\rangle. \tag{11}
\]

For down-core excitations `a=1,2,3,4`, the P/N phase pairs are
`(0,5),(2,6),(3,8),(1,4)`. Edge 7 uses v with both down cores zero.
The runner expands every signed CAR creation word from these definitions.
Their complete Gram is exactly I9. It also decodes address 14336 from
the original bit rule, compares all eighteen coordinates, and verifies all
nine original real positive-Write matrices.

For global flavor `E_ab=sum_x c^dagger_(xa)c_(xb)`, the CAR yields
`[E_ab,E_cd]=delta_bc E_ad-delta_ad E_cb`. The runner checks this on every
state of each complete fiber it uses. Casimir products

\[
 C_2=\sum_{a,b} E_{ab}E_{ba}
\]

keep all intermediate weights. On complete density fibers 42, 33, 321,
their exact projector ranks are `(6)`, `(6,10)`, `(12,20,64)` at labels
`(14)`, `(14,18)`, `(14,18,24)`. The rational spectral projectors are
formed and checked for completeness, idempotence and their eigen-equations
on every fiber basis state. In particular the source 42 projection is
`(C2-18)(C2-24)/40` on its actual supported fibers, not a scalar weight
substitution.

The resulting actual phase42 Gram has, on each P/N pair,

\[
 G_{42}|_{P_a,N_a}=\frac{47}{150}
        \begin{pmatrix}1&1\\1&1\end{pmatrix},\quad
 G_{42,77}=1,
                                                        \tag{12}
\]

with all other blocks zero. The projected source rank is five. Each
projected vector is killed by the raising generators in Weyl flavor order
`(0,2,1,3)`, and its highest weight in that order is `(2,2,1,1)`.
Thus it is five orbital copies of the full flavor irrep
`F2211=det tensor wedge^2(C4)`, of dimension six. The original full600
source definition and its polar convention are inherited operands; its
raw Gram and the needed full-flavor42 coefficients, rank and

Place two prepared contact Li6 sources in distinct cells. Their CAR/Fock
source factors are orthogonal by cell and their raw metric is `M tensor M`
of rank 360000. The full input includes their actual clouds. A coherent
unitary density dressing and a scalar receiving map preserve (12); this
does not price preparing or undressing those inputs.

The product of the two highest 42 vectors is highest of weight `(4,4,2,2)`.
It generates the Cartan product, not all of `6 tensor 6`. The full product
decomposes as dimensions `1+15+20`, after its determinant factors, and the
desired irreducible factor has dimension

\[
 \prod_{i<j}\frac{\lambda_i-\lambda_j+j-i}{j-i}=20,
 \qquad \lambda=(4,4,2,2).                              \tag{13}
\]

The target has exactly the same highest weight: both up supports coincide;
the down supports coincide and are contained in the up supports. Every
raising move is Pauli blocked. The entire fixed-density target flavor
fiber has 36 states, from every two-of-four choice at each partial site.
All sixteen physical generators, including weight-changing moves, give
Casimir labels `36,44,48` and ranks `1,15,20`. The highest selected target
is exactly in label 48; its generated orbit has rank 20. Different target
densities remain orthogonal under every flavor action because flavor
preserves local total density. Thus there are at least 25 full target
copies of the same 20-dimensional irrep.

Here is the complete intertwiner. Orthogonalize the five independent
source42 highest vectors with their exact Gram. Their ordered two-source
products give 25 orthonormal highest vectors `s_i`. Let `t_i` be the 25
selected target highest Slaters, with their coherent clouds. Apply one
ordered list of flavor-lowering words to each s_i and t_i. Inner products
of such words are identical: commute raising operators to the right using
the displayed Lie relations, use their annihilation of the highest
vectors, and use the same highest weight and normalization. Orthogonalize

`a=1,...,20`, are orthonormal full-flavor bases. Define

\[
 Q=\sum_{i,a}|s_{i,a}\rangle\langle s_{i,a}|,\qquad
 R_s=\sum_{i,a}|t_{i,a}\rangle\langle s_{i,a}|.           \tag{14}
\]

Then `R_s*R_s=Q`, `R_sR_s*=P_T`, and it intertwines full U4. This supplies
the full 500-dimensional orbit, not only 25 fixed-weight vectors. Because
the incoming matter has six particles in each of two distinct cells and
the output has twelve in one cell, Q and P_T are orthogonal. Therefore

\[
 U=R_s+R_s^*+I-Q-P_T
                                                        \tag{15}
\]

is an explicit flavor-commuting whole-parent unitary swap. This is a
bounded algebraic definition, not a dense matrix or an implemented circuit.

The complete coherent success/failure dilation is

\[
 \mathcal V\psi=R_sQ\psi\otimes|s\rangle+
                       (I-Q)\psi\otimes|f\rangle.
 \qquad \mathcal V^*\mathcal V=I.                       \tag{16}
\]

The flag is a retained quantum record in the Gram/Write statement.

reversibility of that reduced classical output would be false. Equation
(16), with the complete record retained, is the source-preserving object.

On phase81 the success effect is `S=G42 tensor G42`, and the failure effect

then forms every product eigenvalue. They are
`0 (56), (47/75)^2 (16), 47/75 (8), 1 (1)`. Thus success has rank25,

For the unchanged normalized source
`z=(1,1,-1,-1,-1,1,1,1,1)/3`, the rebuilt contraction is

\[
 z^*G_{42}z=169/675,\qquad
 p_{s}(z\otimes z)=(169/675)^2=28561/455625.              \tag{17}
\]

It is not constant over phase81. For allplus the pair probability is
`203401/455625`; edge7 squared has probability one, and an antisymmetric

For the full polar source isometry F and any original unitary source
Write w, put `J=V F` and

\[
 \widetilde w=JwJ^*+(I-JJ^*).                            \tag{18}
\]

Since `J*J=I`, (18) is unitary, `widetilde w J=Jw`, and every source
Gram and all Write words are retained. This proves the statement for all

In raw coordinates use the same positive metric M tensor M and its polar

The uniform target energy (9) belongs to normalized success outputs.

## 5. B04: supplied-input loading, capacity and location alternative

The downstream estimate starts from a supplied bare selected target and
the free field vacuum. Freeze off-diagonal matter hopping with its declared
control, retaining the constant kinetic diagonal, and ramp the scalar
coupling with `s(t)=t/T`. For fixed density, the exact residual coherent
amplitude above the final equilibrium displacement is

\[
 \eta_q(T)=-ig\rho_q(1-e^{-i\omega_qT})/(T\omega_q^2),
 \quad E_{\rm res}=\frac{4g^2}{T^2}
       \rho^TG^3\sin^2(\omega T/2)\rho.                 \tag{19}
\]

The sign of eta has no effect on its energy. With odd integer frequencies,
`|sin(omega T/2)|<=omega|T-2pi|/2`; hence uniformly on the selected target

\[
 e_0\leq g^2\frac{2632}{105}
                \left(\frac{|T-2\pi|}{T}\right)^2.
                                                        \tag{20}
\]

At `T=25/4`, the runner certifies `|T-2pi|/T<83/15625` with rational
alternating-series enclosures for Machin's identity
`pi=16 atan(1/5)-4 atan(1/239)`. The tangent identity is exact:
`tan(4 atan(1/5))=120/119`, and subtraction of `atan(1/239)` gives one
in the first quadrant. The alternating remainder bounds are signed exact
rationals, not floating error estimates. The resulting (20) is strictly
less than `(4/25)^2`.

Let epsilon_m be the supplied compiler state-norm error, let R(t) be a
bounded **matter** residual during loading, and set
`A_R=int ||R|| dt`, `A=epsilon_m+A_R`. Such R commutes with b. Since
density commutes with the frozen field/coupling law,
`||dot rho||<=2N||R||`. In the Heisenberg picture,

\[
 C(T)=e^{-i\omega T}b(0)+
 g\int_0^T e^{-i\omega(T-u)}G
       [\dot s(u)\rho(u)+s(u)\dot\rho(u)]du,
 \quad C=b+sgG\rho.                                    \tag{21}
\]

The residual R contribution is at most `2|g|N A_R` in field-energy norm:
the first term after subtracting frozen rho contributes weights
`int_(u>=v) dot s(u)du=1-s(v)`, and the second contributes s(v), so their
sum is one. The compiler contribution is at most `|g|N epsilon_m`, since
`||omega^(-1/2)rho||<=N` and the ramp has variation one. Consequently the
safe combined error is `2|g|N A`.

On the entire global fixed-N matter sector,
`0<=T<=12N(tau+t_c)` and
`0<=V<=sum rho_x^2<=4N=48`. Therefore

\[
 S_m=T-g^2V\ \hbox{has spectrum in}
 [-48g^2,144(\tau+t_c)],\quad
 W=144(\tau+t_c)+48g^2.                                 \tag{22}
\]

Duhamel plus the compiler bound gives total vector error at most A from
the ideal loading history. Centering the bounded interval in (22) gives
an S_m expectation defect at most W A. Equations (19)–(22) thus prove

\[
 E_{\rm actual}\leq U_{25}+W A+
                       (\sqrt{e_0}+2|g|N A)^2.          \tag{23}
\]

This is a field-energy-form bound plus a bounded matter bound; it never
uses a norm of unbounded full H. At the benchmark, `A<=1/200`, `|g|<6`
and `sqrt(e0)<4/25` yield exactly the conservative error
`123689/12500` and allowance `25411/12500` after subtracting it from
`1491/125`.

Equations (19)–(23) do not begin with two incoming dressed Li6 sources.
They do not pay projection, the new orbital map, incoming cloud changes,
arrival, clock preparation, work-store correlations, loss or reset. They
are discrete eight-mode formulas; no continuum recurrence is inferred.
Every complete-reference comparison must telescope actual state energies
and apparatus/heat changes under the same H and zero. A conditional
downstream allowance is not that complete formation ledger.

For the one-cell capacity statement, the runner computes the whole

Nor is it an unrestricted full-Fock information-capacity theorem: V

A changed receiver can use 24 maximal profiles in cell0 and one maximal

## What establishes the result

The proof derives the spectrum transform, forced energy form and box identity.
For T/(2*tau)=n+r, the residue fibers contain n+1 or n+2 available boxes whose
signed masses sum to one. Cauchy gives

    E_res/D_jump >= (1-r)/(n+1) + r/(n+2).

The positive two-grid schedule has exactly equal mass on every available
fiber and therefore attains the lower; merging endpoint coincidences gives
the integer schedules. This is an all-measure proof. The finite replay is
an independent implementation check rather than its replacement.

The new exact host program integrates its own piecewise-affine box loads and
solves the stationary equations for schedule weights over rational numbers.
It reconstructs all 5 supplied original finite schedules after solving,
then checks their entire residue intervals and endpoint cases. It also
reconstructs the linear, rate-pulse and smoothed energies independently.
Earlier generator code is only a read-only reference/hash operand and is
never imported or executed.

The directed replay checks the original full spectrum separately: finite
Acb analytic integration plus a rigorous infinite-tail bound. All
11 full-spectrum checks pass. The signed-control full integral is enclosed
approximately by 0.6229742202451053 to
0.6428685881315922, containing its exact energy and having a
lower endpoint above the sharp lower. Authoritative bounds are the exact
rational endpoints in [DIRECTED_SPECTRUM.json](output/DIRECTED_SPECTRUM.json);
the displayed decimals are diagnostic only.

The LC04 Fock/control findings are analytic domain results and documentary
premise checks. The output booleans do not substitute for those proofs.
The original source law, metric and Gray-event normalization are inherited
operands, with original and copy hashes retained in
[SOURCE_CUSTODY.json](SOURCE_CUSTODY.json). The one missing public causal
report was recovered and sealed before arithmetic.

## Reference and formation scope

The field residual is above the final completed-square minimum. It is
unchanged by a scalar counterterm, while inclusive work obeys

    W = Delta C + (c_1-D_1) - (c_0-D_0) + E_res.

The original endpoint law has c_i=D_i. A coupling-off free Hamiltonian and a
vertex-only switch-off that retains the endpoint +D scalar have different
incoming baselines; that choice must be stated before assigning signed work.
The counterterm-free discrete attraction law is a distinct account.

The valid normal-Fock construction starts with free vacuum and a supplied
scalar drive toward one fixed legal endpoint source. Intermediate forces do
not create legal fractional C4 phases. Nonzero static shell displacement is
an equilibrium energy-form/Gaussian sector with logarithmically divergent
normal photon norm; an arbitrary normalized incoming field needs its actual
energy and interference cross term. Unequal radii require their actual
summed kernels and a joint control account.

No physical controller/clock, nuclear rate, incoming equilibrium preparation,
matter rearrangement/arrival, additional control, recording, work-store
restoration or reusable reset is supplied here. These are identified operands
for a formation join; they are not inferred payments or new theorem premises.
No CAR/flavor result from the other two tracks is borrowed.

# Independent reconstruction of the scalar shell-loading statements

The whole-continuum lower and its attaining ramps follow from a box integral,
with no finite-mode approximation. The initial state and actuator remain
operands of the theorem. This derivation reconstructs C01–C04 and supplies the
analytic part of LC01–LC04. The replay checks the formulas separately by exact
rational integration, independently solved stationary equations, and directed
integration of the original continuum spectrum.

## 1. Original source, spectrum and energy form — C01

The retained source law is

\[
 F_a=H_0-\Phi(g_a)+D_a,\qquad
 H_0=d\Gamma(I_*\omega),\quad
 g_a=\sqrt{I_*\omega/2}\,a,\quad D_a=\tfrac12\|a\|^2.
 \tag{1}
\]

Here \(I_*>0\), \(R,c>0\), \(\tau=R/c\), and the source is frozen during
the loading calculation. All forced polarizations and spatial source channels
belong to the norm in (1); none is discarded. In the common-radius reduction
the complete whitened displacement satisfies

\[
 \|\Delta a\|^2=\sum_r\Delta u^T G_r\Delta u,
 \qquad D_{\rm jump}=\tfrac12\|\Delta a\|^2.
 \tag{2}
\]

This is the given source metric, not a fitted scalar replacement. For a
representative common spectral factor one can write
\(a(\omega)=A\sqrt{\tau p(\omega\tau)}\) in a direct-sum one-particle space,
with the complete channel norm \(\|A\|^2\). Only the resulting spectral
norm measure is needed:

\[
 x=\omega\tau,\qquad
 p(x)=\frac2\pi\left(\frac{\sin x}{x}\right)^2,\quad x\geq0,
 \qquad \int_0^\infty p(x)\,dx=1.
 \tag{3}
\]

The value at zero is continuous. This is the entire original shell spectrum.
Finite support used below describes a control measure, never the field.

For completeness, derive its transform without using the asserted triangle.
Let \(J(b)=\int_0^\infty(1-\cos bx)x^{-2}dx\). Integration by parts and
the Dirichlet integral give \(J(b)=\pi|b|/2\). The latter integral follows
by first inserting \(e^{-\epsilon x}\), differentiating with respect to
\(b\), and integrating \(\epsilon/(\epsilon^2+b^2)\) from zero to \(b\);
the result is \(\arctan(b/\epsilon)\), tending to \(\pi/2\) for \(b>0\).
Integration-by-parts tail bounds justify this Abel limit. The convergent
combination of the three \(J\)'s therefore yields

\[
\begin{aligned}
 \int_0^\infty p(x)\cos(zx)dx
 &=\frac{J(z+2)+J(z-2)-2J(z)}{2\pi}\\
 &=\frac{|z+2|+|z-2|-2|z|}{4}
 =\left(1-\frac{|z|}{2}\right)_+.
\end{aligned}\tag{4}
\]

At \(z=0\) this also proves the normalization in (3). The construction
retains the hard-shell ultraviolet tail and the nonzero infrared density.

Write the formal equilibrium displacement
\(h_a=g_a/(I_*\omega)=a/\sqrt{2I_*\omega}\). For a normal coherent state
with amplitude \(f\) of finite free energy, square completion is an identity
of energy forms:

\[
 \langle F_a\rangle_f
 =\int I_*\omega|f-h_a|^2d\omega\geq0.
 \tag{5}
\]

The notation does not assert \(h_a\) belongs to the one-particle Hilbert
space. The linear field is well defined as an energy form since
\(g_a/\sqrt{I_*\omega}=a/\sqrt2\in\mathfrak h\). At the hard-shell
ultraviolet endpoint \(g_a\) itself need not be in \(\mathfrak h\); (1)
should not be silently interpreted as a smeared creation operator on the
finite-particle core with an \(L^2\) smearing function. The positive form
in (5), its closed energy-form realization, and finite-time normal Weyl
differences are the appropriate objects. Frequency cutoffs give (5) first;
their energy-form limit keeps precisely these finite quantities.

The infimum in (5) is zero: approximate \(h_a\) by its restrictions to
\(\epsilon\leq\omega\leq L\). Their energy-norm difference tends to zero
because \(a\in\mathfrak h\). For a nonzero shell source the infimum is not
a normal coherent ground vector, as established in Section 6.

## 2. Forced solution and exact residual — C01

Let \(\mu\) be a finite real signed Borel measure on \([0,T]\) with
\(\mu([0,T])=1\). Thus \(s(t)=\mu([0,t])\) is a bounded-variation real
scalar control, with value zero before zero and one after \(T\). Endpoint
atoms are included. Bounded variation is the meaning of finite signed ramp
here; distributions of infinite total variation are outside this theorem.

For the clean normal-Fock case, start in the free vacuum, with the field
coupling off, and supply the force \(s(t)a_1\) of one fixed legal endpoint
source. Intermediate force strengths are external actuator settings. They
are not newly legal fractional C4 matter states. The classical scalar
force fixes the coherent amplitude through

\[
 \dot f_t=-i\omega f_t+i\omega s(t)h_{a_1},\qquad f_{0-}=0.
 \tag{6}
\]

Integration by parts in the Stieltjes sense, including both endpoint
atoms, gives at and after the terminal drive change

\[
 f_T=h_{a_1}\left[1-e^{-i\omega T}\widehat\mu(\omega)\right],
 \qquad \widehat\mu(\omega)=\int_{[0,T]}e^{i\omega u}\mu(du).
 \tag{7}
\]

For example, \(\mu=\delta_T\) leaves \(f_T=0\): a final instantaneous
force change does not move the field. An atom at zero has the full elapsed
evolution. Both contribute to the residual relative to the final force.

Equation (7) is a normal finite-energy coherent amplitude. Indeed,

\[
 1-e^{-i\omega T}\widehat\mu(\omega)
 =\int[1-e^{-i\omega(T-u)}]\mu(du)
\]

is bounded by \(\|\mu\|_{\rm TV}\min(2,\omega T)\). This suppresses the
infrared singularity of \(h\), while \(p(x)/x=O(x^{-3})\) controls the
ultraviolet photon norm. Its energy is finite because \(p\) is integrable.
The associated Weyl displacement is unitary on normal Fock space. Its scalar
phase does not change (5); it must still be retained when comparing coherent
source histories or transported Writes.

For a transition between two legal nonzero endpoint drives, supplied in the
equilibrium energy-form sector of \(a_0\), the same oscillator calculation
has deviation \(-e^{-i\omega T}\Delta h\widehat\mu\) from \(h_{a_1}\).
Consequently in either precisely stated case,

\[
\begin{aligned}
 E_{\rm res}
 &=D_{\rm jump}\int_0^\infty p(x)
       \left|\int e^{ixt/\tau}\mu(dt)\right|^2dx\\
 &=D_{\rm jump}\iint
       \left(1-\frac{|t-u|}{2\tau}\right)_+\mu(dt)\mu(du).
\end{aligned}\tag{8}
\]

The real signed condition ensures that the imaginary antisymmetric cross
terms cancel; the two ordered real mixed terms remain. Fubini is valid:
the absolute integral is bounded by \(\|\mu\|_{\rm TV}^2\int p\).
It is an exact whole-continuum energy-form identity, not an assertion from
sampling the spectrum. In particular no photon cutoff is used by (8).
The normalized ratio is used when \(D_{\rm jump}>0\), as for the supplied
nontrivial source/Gray displacement. If \(\Delta a=0\), the unnormalized
identity gives \(E_{\rm res}=0\); no strictly positive energy is asserted
for a zero drive. The normalized control functional below remains defined
independently of that trivial source case.

An arbitrary normalized incoming state cannot replace the specified vacuum
or equilibrium sector. For a coherent incoming amplitude \(f_0\), the
general deviation at the end is

\[
 f_T-h_{a_1}=e^{-i\omega T}
 \big[(f_0-h_{a_0})-\Delta h\widehat\mu(\omega)\big].
 \tag{9}
\]

Its square includes the incoming energy and its signed cross term with the
drive. For general states the corresponding mean, covariance and energy
form are required. An equilibrium Gaussian sector with fluctuations retains
their energy/reference separately; (8) is the additional coherent loading
excess, not its total thermal energy. Thus “a normalized finite-energy
preparation” alone is insufficient to infer (8).

## 3. Sharp lower for all finite signed ramps — C02

Put \(a=2\tau\), \(L=T/a\), and write \(L=n+r\) with integer \(n\geq0\)
and \(0\leq r<1\). The alternate endpoint representation \(r=1\) is also
valid and gives the same value as \((n+1,0)\). Let

\[
 m(x)=\mu((x-a,x]),\qquad K_a(v)=(1-|v|/a)_+.
\]

The intersection of two intervals of length \(a\) has length
\((a-|t-u|)_+\). Therefore the box autocorrelation and signed Fubini give

\[
 Q(\mu):=E_{\rm res}/D_{\rm jump}
 =\frac1a\int_{\mathbb R}m(x)^2dx.
 \tag{10}
\]

Here \(|m|\leq\|\mu\|_{\rm TV}\) and its essential support is
\([0,T+a]\), so the energy is finite. Using closed intervals in both
places changes \(m\) only at a Lebesgue-null set of atom endpoints. The
half-open convention above makes the following partition literal.

Disintegrate the Lebesgue integral as

\[
 \int m(x)^2dx=\int_0^a\sum_{j\in\mathbb Z}m(\theta+ja)^2d\theta.
 \tag{11}
\]

For each residue \(\theta\), the intervals
\((\theta+(j-1)a,\theta+ja]\) partition the line. Their signed masses
sum to one. Apart from the finitely many boundary residues that do not
affect (11), the only available slots are

\[
 \begin{cases}
 j=0,\ldots,n+1,&0<\theta<ra,\\
 j=0,\ldots,n,&ra<\theta<a.
 \end{cases}
\]

Some masses may be zero or negative; Cauchy still gives
\(\sum_{j=1}^N m_j^2\geq(\sum m_j)^2/N=1/N\). Integrating those two
families proves the all-measure lower

\[
 \boxed{Q(\mu)\geq q_*(L):=\frac{1-r}{n+1}+\frac r{n+2}>0.}
 \tag{12}
\]

There is no monotonicity premise in this argument. Finite total variation,
real scalar drive, compact time support and mass one are its control
hypotheses. At \(T=0\), the only measure is \(\delta_0\) and \(Q=1\).
At \(T=na\), the exceptional residue is zero and (12) is \(1/(n+1)\).
At \(r=1\), all nonboundary fibers have \(n+2\) slots and the answer is
\(1/(n+2)\), consistently.

The exact deficit form is also useful:

\[
 Q(\mu)-q_*(L)=\frac1a\int_0^a\sum_{j=1}^{N(\theta)}
       [m_j(\theta)-1/N(\theta)]^2d\theta.
 \tag{13}
\]

It locates any failure of equality without relying on a numerical optimizer.

## 4. Attainment, uniqueness and integer endpoint controls — C02, LC01–LC02

For \(0<r<1\), use atoms at \(ka\) and \((k+r)a\), \(k=0,\ldots,n\),
with masses

\[
 A_k=\frac{n+1-k}{(n+1)(n+2)},\qquad
 B_k=\frac{k+1}{(n+1)(n+2)}.
 \tag{14}
\]

All are positive and their sum is one. On \(0<\theta<ra\), slot \(j\)
contains \(A_j+B_{j-1}=1/(n+2)\), with nonexistent end terms zero. On
\(ra<\theta<a\), it contains \(A_j+B_j=1/(n+1)\). These are exactly
the equality loads of (13), so (14) attains (12) on every required residue
interval, not only at its atom locations.

At \(r=0\), merge \(A_k+B_k=1/(n+1)\) at \(ka\). At \(r=1\), merge
\(A_k+B_{k-1}=1/(n+2)\) at \(ka\), including the two outer endpoints.
Thus the integer-time minimizer is \(n+1\) equal increments separated by
\(2\tau\). The terminal atom is part of the unit-mass ramp even though
it has no subsequent evolution at the instant of measurement.

For uniqueness, the quadratic form is strictly positive on any nonzero
finite signed measure of compact support: by (8), zero energy would force
its continuous Fourier transform to vanish almost everywhere on the
positive line since \(p>0\) except at isolated points. Continuity makes it
zero everywhere there. The Fourier transform is entire for compact support,
so all its moments vanish; polynomial density on the compact time interval
then makes the measure zero. Two distinct minimizers would have a midpoint
with strictly lower energy by this strict positivity. Hence (14), with
endpoint merging, is the unique minimizer in the full finite signed class.

The replay reconstructs its weights independently: on the two breakpoint
grids it solves the exact constant-potential stationary equations with a
unit-mass constraint. It then checks the whole residue deficit (13), the
box integral and the submitted formulas separately. Agreement of finite
examples is an implementation check; (10)–(14) are the proof for every time.

Concrete LC01: at \(T=a\), \((\delta_0+\delta_a)/2\) has \(Q=1/2\),
whereas a unit-mass linear ramp has \(Q=2/3\). Omitting the terminal atom
would leave mass \(1/2\) and energy \(1/4\); that apparent improvement
fails the mass-one premise. Renormalizing the retained atom gives \(Q=1\).

Concrete LC02: at \(T=a\), take

\[
 \mu=\tfrac34\delta_0-\tfrac12\delta_{a/2}+\tfrac34\delta_a.
\]

Its cumulative control drops at \(a/2\), but its mass is one. Directly,
\(Q=9/16+1/4+9/16-3/4=5/8\). Formula (13) gives the exact positive
deficit \(1/8\) above \(1/2\). Signed excursions do not bypass the lower.

## 5. Rates and approximations — C03

For a linear ramp \(\mu(dt)=dt/T\), direct difference integration gives

\[
 Q_{\rm lin}=\frac2{T^2}\int_0^{\min(T,a)}(T-v)(1-v/a)dv
 =\begin{cases}
 1-T/(3a),&0<T\leq a,\\
 a/T-a^2/(3T^2),&T\geq a.
 \end{cases}\tag{15}
\]

The two formulas meet at \(2/3\). The limit at \(T\downarrow0\) is one.
This is \(1-T/(6\tau)\) and
\(2\tau/T-4\tau^2/(3T^2)\), respectively. The replay derives each finite
case from the square of its actual piecewise-affine box load, independently
of (15).

For the rate statement the precise extra assumptions are absolute
continuity and \(0\leq s'(t)\leq M\), with
\(1/M\leq T\leq a\). A signed derivative with only an absolute bound is
a different constrained problem, not asserted here. Set \(F(t)=s(t)\).
The distance identity follows from integrating the indicator that one of
two independently drawn control times lies below \(t\) and the other above:

\[
 \mathbb E|X-Y|=2\int_0^T F(t)[1-F(t)]dt,
 \quad Q=1-\mathbb E|X-Y|/a.
 \tag{16}
\]

The slope constraint forces

\[
 \max(0,1-M(T-t))\leq F(t)\leq\min(1,Mt).
 \tag{17}
\]

The concave integrand \(F(1-F)\) is maximal at \(1/2\). Clipping this
value into (17) is feasible and gives

\[
 F_*(t)=\begin{cases}
 Mt,&0\leq t\leq1/(2M),\\
 1/2,&1/(2M)\leq t\leq T-1/(2M),\\
 1-M(T-t),&T-1/(2M)\leq t\leq T.
 \end{cases}
\]

It pointwise maximizes (16). The first and last integrals of
\(F(1-F)\) are each \(1/(12M)\); the middle is
\((T-1/M)/4\). Consequently

\[
 \boxed{Q_{M,*}=1-\frac{T}{2a}+\frac1{6aM}
 =1-\frac{T}{4\tau}+\frac1{12\tau M}.}
 \tag{18}
\]

Its density is \(M\) on the first and last intervals of length
\(1/(2M)\), zero in between. At \(T=1/M\), the intervals meet and give
the uniform ramp. This is attainment in the bounded measurable-density
class; the jumps of its density are not a claim of smooth attainment.
For \(T<1/M\) there is no feasible mass-one density. No formula for
\(T>a\) under a fixed rate cap is asserted.

For smooth approximation of the unrestricted atomic optimum at fixed
\(T>0\), choose \(0<h<T\). Map each atom time to
\(c_h(t)=h/2+(1-h/T)t\), then convolve it with a nonnegative normalized
bump supported in \([-h/2,h/2]\). Every resulting time lies in \([0,T]\)
and differs from its original time by at most \(h\). Since \(K_a\) is
\(1/a\)-Lipschitz, a product coupling gives

\[
 0\leq Q(\mu_h)-q_*(T/a)\leq2h/a.
 \tag{19}
\]

For an explicit smooth bump use a normalized dilation of
\(\eta(v)=C\exp[-1/(1-v^2)]\mathbf1_{|v|<1}\). Its finite maximum gives
\(\|s'_h\|_\infty\leq 2\|\eta\|_\infty/h\). Thus smooth ramps with
finite rates individually approach the value as their permitted rate
increases. One fixed finite rate cap is not asserted to approach the atomic
infimum. Uniqueness in Section 4 prevents any absolutely continuous ramp
from attaining it. There is no positive-duration smooth ramp at \(T=0\).
The replay uses uniform bumps instead to check (19) exactly; these yield
bounded piecewise-constant densities, with the C-infinity regularity supplied
by the written bump construction rather than inferred from the computation.

## 6. Normal Fock space, supplied sectors and complete reference — C04, LC04

For a nonzero common shell amplitude,

\[
 \|h_a\|^2=\frac{\|A\|^2\tau}{2I_*}
       \int_0^\infty \frac{p(x)}x dx=\infty.
 \tag{20}
\]

Indeed for \(0<x\leq1\), \(\sin x/x\geq1-x^2/6\geq5/6\), so
\(p(x)\geq25/(18\pi)\). The integral has at least this positive multiple
of \(\int_0^1dx/x\). Its energy norm remains finite because
\(\int p=1\). Thus the exact static equilibrium shift is a displaced
energy-form/Gaussian sector, not a vector obtained by a normal Fock Weyl
operator. This concerns the nonzero original common shell profile. A
different source whose complete spectrum vanishes sufficiently rapidly in
the infrared must be evaluated with its actual norm; its behavior is not
inferred from (20).

Nor can a different normal Fock vector attain the zero infimum. Vanishing
of the completed-square form forces the annihilation eigenvalue equations
\(b(k)\psi=\langle k,h_a\rangle\psi\) for every test function supported in
a finite frequency band away from zero. The Fock-sector recursion for these
equations fixes its components to
\(\psi_j=\psi_0 h_a^{\otimes j}/\sqrt{j!}\) on every such band. If
\(\psi_0=0\), the same recursion makes every component zero. Otherwise
normalization requires the coherent sum
\(|\psi_0|^2\exp(\|h_a\|^2)\) to be finite, contrary to (20). Equivalently,
the compatible finite-band coherent vacua have no normal Fock limit. This
establishes the missing normal equilibrium vector without restricting the
conclusion to a proposed coherent ansatz.

By contrast the finite-time difference in (7) is normal. Thus the valid
free-vacuum supplied-drive case survives LC04. A claimed incoming nonzero
static *normal Fock vacuum* does not. A supplied conditional Gaussian sector
is an explicit alternative premise, and arbitrary incoming normal states
require (9). These are distinct initial preparations.

A source counterterm is a scalar energy term. It does not change (6)–(8),
but it changes work and the complete reference. For an arbitrary stated
scalar counterterm \(c(t)\), the completed-square infimum at the endpoint
source \(a_i\) is

\[
 C_i+c_i-D_i,
\]

where \(C_i\) is the actual matter/source endpoint energy. With an incoming
equilibrium energy form and no additional fluctuation change, inclusive
prescribed work is

\[
 W=(C_1-C_0)+(c_1-D_1)-(c_0-D_0)+E_{\rm res}.
 \tag{21}
\]

For the original endpoint law \(c_i=D_i\), this is
\(\Delta C+E_{\rm res}\). For a counterterm-free receiving law it is
\(\Delta C-\Delta D+E_{\rm res}\). Their static references differ.
The law in (1) must not be silently replaced by the attractive law of the
eight massive discrete receiver modes.

There is one practical convention to make explicit for the added
coupling-off control. If it means the free law \(H_0\), its scalar baseline
is \(c_0=0=D_0\); if it means suppressing only the vertex while retaining
the endpoint's \(+D_1\) scalar term, then \(c_0=D_1\) and the incoming
energy is \(D_1\). Both give (8) for the final excess, but (21) gives
different signed work by that known baseline. A complete preparation ledger
must state which Hamiltonian implements “off.” No choice of this scalar
term improves the residual minimum. This clarification is recorded so a
later formation join can use its actual supplied controller reference.

In (21) the source-drive interpolation, its counterterm and known phase
are external prescribed data. No controller Hamiltonian, clock error,
switching rate in seconds, source-motion work, source compiler, incoming
cloud preparation, measurement/failure record, work-store restoration or
reusable reset is calculated by (8). These are additional operands for a
physical formation account, not changes to the mathematical result.

An independently variable momentum quadrature or frequency-dependent drive
changes (6). Its Hamiltonian/domain and inclusive work must be supplied
before judging its performance. In particular a formal counterdiabatic
drive proportional to \(\dot h_a\) is not automatically a normal-Fock
control, because (20) still applies. This track neither prices nor assumes
such an actuator. Relabeling an unpriced additional drive as the same scalar
ramp violates the frozen control class; it is the second LC04 premise check.

## 7. Local means, discrete cancellation and source-clock join — C03–C04, LC03

For \(t\geq T\), the evolved free-vacuum amplitude is
\(h_a[1-e^{-i\omega t}\widehat\mu(\omega)]\). Its projection onto the
same complete source drive has

\[
 \frac{\langle\Phi(g_a)\rangle_t}{D}
 =2[1-B(t)],\qquad
 B(t)=\int K_a(t-u)\mu(du).
 \tag{22}
\]

Its full free field energy is

\[
 \langle H_0\rangle_t/D=1+Q(\mu)-2B(t).
 \tag{23}
\]

At \(t\geq T+a\), \(B(t)=0\) because the self-response has finite
support, including its zero boundary value. The local drive mean has then
its equilibrium value \(2D\), while (23) is \(D(1+Q)\). Including the
vertex and original counterterm leaves \(DQ>0\). Other receivers have
their own exact response horizons; no universal all-local-observable
horizon is substituted. Once the final source is fixed, common unitary
evolution preserves complete state overlaps and the outgoing record.

For the LC01 optimum at \(T=a\), observation at \(t=2a\) has
\(\langle\Phi\rangle=2D\), \(\langle H_0\rangle=3D/2\), and
\(\langle F_a\rangle=D/2\). Thus settled local mean and zero full energy
are distinct checks, exactly as LC03 requests.

The original discrete receiver has frequencies \(1,3,5,7\), with full
bosonic Fock spaces at those finitely many one-particle modes. The half-step
measure \((\delta_0+\delta_\pi)/2\) has Fourier value
\((1+(-1)^w)/2=0\) at every such frequency. It therefore cancels the
discrete residual exactly. For the original continuum with \(\tau=1\),
the same half steps have delay \(\pi>2\tau\), so their off-diagonal
triangular overlap is zero and their full residual is \(D/2\). Likewise
a linear ramp of duration \(2\pi\) cancels those discrete modes, but its
continuum residual is \(D[1/\pi-1/(3\pi^2)]>0\). No finite signed
unit-mass control has Fourier transform zero on the entire positive
continuum, by (12) or by the continuity argument in Section 4.

The supplied original Gray event has squared receiver change two and
\(D_{\rm jump}=G_{\rm feedback}/R=\epsilon_{\rm phase}\), by the full
self metric and the factor \(1/2\) in (2). The source normalization

\[
 \epsilon_{\rm phase}\tau/I=\alpha\gamma_{\rm feedback}
\]

is inherited from the sealed causal report; no physical MeV or seconds are
fit here. Conditional use of (12) at \(T=2n\tau\) yields

\[
 E_{\rm res}=\frac{\epsilon_{\rm phase}}{n+1},\qquad
 \frac{E_{\rm res}T}{I}
 =2\alpha\gamma_{\rm feedback}\frac n{n+1}.
 \tag{24}
\]

This is a prescribed source/control relation. For a nonzero-to-nonzero
Gray event the incoming equilibrium-sector condition remains essential;
it does not supply a normal-Fock equilibrium preparation or a nuclear
transition rate. For different channel radii the functional is the sum
\(\sum_rD_r\iint K_{2\tau_r}(t-u)\mu(dt)\mu(du)\). The one-radius
proof does not assert one schedule minimizes that sum.

## 8. Executable evidence and comparisons

All quantities in the replay use dimensionless \(y=t/a\). The independent
box integrator constructs its own breakpoints and integrates the square of
the affine load exactly. The independent schedule solver uses rational
Gaussian elimination of the unit-mass stationary equations, then verifies
every residue interval and all five original submitted schedules. It does
not call or import the original generator. These are exact host checks.

The directed checks independently integrate \(p(x)\) times the actual
Fourier products, using analytic Acb integration over \([0,X]\). The
entire sinc function avoids a removable pole at zero. Since

\[
 p(x)\leq2/(\pi x^2),\quad
 |\widehat\mu(x)|\leq\|\mu\|_{\rm TV},\quad
 \int_X^\infty p(x)|\widehat\mu|^2dx
 \leq\frac{2\|\mu\|_{\rm TV}^2}{\pi X},
\]

the final energy enclosure adds a nonnegative rigorously bounded tail.
The cosine-transform checks use a two-sided tail of \(2/(\pi X)\).
No finite integration cutoff is promoted to a field cutoff. The enclosures
must contain each exact independently computed result, remain finite and
have the preregistered maximum width. The signed control's lower directed
endpoint must itself exceed the sharp \(1/2\) lower.

The Fock/initial-state and controller checks are analytic domain arguments
and documentary premise fixtures; no output boolean is offered as a new
proof of the domain theorem. The finite examples likewise do not replace
the all-measure arguments (10)–(14) or the all-density argument (16)–(18).

Next use is a lead replay from a clean relocated public case and review of
the supplied initial-state and counterterm conventions before any formation
work account is joined to this theorem.

# Exact hypotheses and evidence map

These are the frozen theorem's operands and the scope of its reconstruction.
They are not additional physical requirements imposed on the other tracks.

| ID | Required operands | Established here | Changes that require a different account |
|---|---|---|---|
| C01 | Original positive-counterterm field law, complete whitened displacement norm, common original shell spectrum p(x), I*>0 and tau=R/c>0; fixed source and a supplied real scalar BV control | Exact forced solution, triangular residual, energy normalization and valid incoming-field cases | A different spectrum, omitted channel, changing matter source, unspecified incoming field, unequal radii reduced to one radius |
| C02 | Finite real signed Borel measure on [0,T], finite T>=0, mass one and finite total variation; endpoint atoms allowed | All-measure residue-fiber lower; unique positive atomic attainment; integer and T=0 endpoints | Infinite-variation generalized controls, missing final atom/mass, independently controlled source directions |

| C03 smoothing | T>0, positive normalized smoothing inside [0,T]; rate allowed to increase as width shrinks | Exact transport error <=2h/(2*tau); C-infinity construction; no continuous-density attainment | Claim of atomic attainment by a finite bounded density; smoothing at T=0 |
| C04 | Free vacuum with coupling off, or an explicitly supplied equilibrium energy-form/Gaussian sector with its fluctuations/reference; complete source counterterm; explicit endpoint baseline | Normal finite-time Weyl differences, nonnormal nonzero static common-shell shift, inclusive reference formula and list of actual formation operands | A static displaced incoming state called a normal Fock vacuum; arbitrary normalized incoming state treated as equilibrium; unpriced extra quadrature/frequency controls |

The clean normal-Fock initial case is the free vacuum with an externally
switched fixed legal endpoint drive. The coupling-off setting is not a new
legal nuclear phase. A nonzero-to-nonzero source transition uses the stated
equilibrium energy-form sector, or else its incoming energy/cross terms must
be evaluated from the actual state. A thermal Gaussian adds its own unchanged
fluctuation energy/reference to the coherent residual; no finite thermal
total is inferred merely from the word Gaussian.
Normalized energies and strict positive excess use a nonzero displacement
D_jump>0. The zero-displacement identity is E_res=0 and its unnormalized lower
is trivial; the all-control functional itself is still well defined.

The original endpoint counterterm is D(a)=||a||^2/2. The residual is above
the final completed-square infimum. Inclusive work additionally uses the
actual scalar c_i and matter/source C_i:

    W = Delta C + (c_1-D_1) - (c_0-D_0) + E_res.

If “coupling off” means the free Hamiltonian, c_0=D_0=0. If only the vertex
is switched off while the endpoint scalar +D_1 is retained, c_0=D_1 and its
incoming baseline is paid. No counterterm convention is inferred from the
same finite-time coherent evolution.

The inherited quantities are the original field law, source metric, common
shell spectral law, and Gray-event normalization D_jump=epsilon_phase with
epsilon_phase*tau/I=alpha*gamma_feedback. Their original reports are sealed
in SOURCE_CUSTODY.json. The spectrum transform, full-control optimization,
rate and domain arguments are reconstructed in DERIVATION.md. All five prior
finite attaining examples are compared after independent exact solution;
their code is neither run nor imported.

No physical controller, seconds assignment, complete incoming nuclear cloud,
source-motion process, apparatus construction, record/reset cost or stable
released final state is supplied by this track. No CAR/flavor result from the
